Shi Ping 石平 — 《儒道》序 · A Preface

Chairman, URIT.com 桂林优利特医疗电子有限公司

TonyTong 唐仪 先生撰《儒道 RuDao》一书，融儒道古学，观照AI时代的商业变革，立论新颖，启人深思。承蒙相邀，嘱我作序，展卷品读，心有戚戚，谨抒拙见，置于卷首。

世间论商者，多援引《孙子兵法》，将商场类比沙场。诚然，《孙子》审时度势、知止慎战之智，可资经营参考。然兵者，国之大事，死生之地，存亡之道，征伐本是不得已而为之，其内核是零和博弈，不可直接移植作为商业立身之本。

民间谓营商为"生意"，二字藏深意:所谓生意，乃是生生不息，创造价值。商业本是社会日常活动，核心在于为上下游伙伴、服务对象增益价值。市场竞争，并非同业之间你死我活的对决；真正的高下之分，在于谁能为客户创造更多、更好、更受社会认同的价值，评判权，终究归于消费者。经营追求共生，而非攻伐；贵在共赢，而非制胜。

此书以儒家立本心，以道家为运用，阐扬"商业向善 无为而治"的儒商正道。道家之"无为"，绝非怠惰躺平，而是摒除机心诡算，不妄为、不贪求短期浮利。经营者循事物本然之理，建良制、厚信任、育生态，守仁义礼智信的底线，明义以生利、义利合一的道理。

今AI浪潮奔涌，技术极大释放生产力，提升效率。但工具愈强大，愈离不开人文的引领与人心的温度。AI精于推演、运算、筹策，却与生俱来没有良知与价值坚守。若仅以新技术作为博弈逐利之器，商业终将沦为冰冷的算计。

技术为器，向善为道。越是技术狂飙的时代，越要坚守有思想、有温度，持续造福人类的经营理念。商业向善，向上而行，此乃颠扑不破之大道，亦是亘古恒存之商道。

愿阅此书者，跳出"商场即战场"的固有执念，汲取儒道和合的智慧，守正笃行，于世事经营之中葆守初心，创造长久的社会价值。

是为序。

— Shi Ping 石平 · 丙午年秋 于桂林 Guilin China · 桂林优利特医疗电子有限公司 URIT.com

Prof. Timmy Tan 陳建榮

HKBA GCSA Manila, Philippines · Son of Lucio Tan

I am Timmy Tan, son of Lucio Tan, Chairman of the Federation of Filipino-Chinese Chambers of Commerce and Industry.

I have known the author Tony Tong for more than twenty years. I first met Tony when he was working with Pacificnet, a Web1 e-commerce company that Tony founded and took public on NASDAQ in 2000. This year, I joined HKBA and its strategic-alliance partner, Global CyberSecurity Alliance (GCSA.ai), as the Managing Partner for the Philippines.

My father Lucio Tan is a steadfast, lifelong practitioner of Ru-Dao and Confucian RuShang philosophy. He firmly lives by the principle of Righteousness before Profit (义利合一) and continuously draws wisdom from the Dao De Jing (道德经), advocating coopetition and harmonious collaborative development. Across his decades building a global business empire spanning banking, aviation, tobacco, and beer, my father always believed that much of his lasting success came directly from Ru-Dao thinking.

During the 2015 APEC summit in Manila, Tony Tong met my father at my corporate gala dinner. My father shared that from childhood, he formed a lifelong habit of studying and memorizing traditional Chinese literary and philosophical classics, including the Analects (论语), The Art of War (孙子兵法), the Three-Character Classic (三字经), the 300 Tang Poems (唐诗300首), and the Romance of the Three Kingdoms (三国演义).

In 2002, my father was invited to deliver a keynote speech at the First International Confucian-Business Forum in Beijing. In his landmark address titled "Integrity is the Foundation for Establishing and Developing Enterprises," Lucio Tan stated that Cheng Xin (诚信), or trustworthiness and integrity, is the unshakable foundation of every enterprise. Even amid severe business crises, my father insisted that integrity must never be traded away for short-term survival.

Today's AI-Web3-blockchain world is filled with distributed remote teams, digital-nomad communities, cross-border partners, and AI digital-avatar collaborators who may never physically meet. Building durable trust for long-distance cross-border alliances has become one of the greatest challenges facing global entrepreneurs.

Tony Tong's new book, RuDao, brilliantly transforms the real, lived RuShang practice of my father into actionable modern-day guidance. It does not merely quote ancient texts. Instead, it connects age-old Chinese entrepreneurial wisdom to the real-world pain points of digital-era coopetition and remote-alliance building. Every founder, investor, and cross-border alliance-builder should read this work.

我是 陈建荣 TimmyTan 菲华商联总会理事长陈永栽之子。

我认识本书 RuDao 作者唐仪 TonyTong.cc 已经超过二十年。当年唐仪创办 Web1 时代创新电商企业 Pacificnet，并于 2000 年登陆纳斯达克上市，我便加入香港区块链协会 HKBA 及其战略联盟伙伴全球网络安全联盟 GCSA.ai，担任菲律宾区管理合伙人。

家父陈永栽（Lucio Tan）是儒道思想与儒家儒商理念坚定的终身践行者。家父恪守义利合一,不断从《道德经》汲取智慧，倡导竞合与和谐协作发展。数十年间，陈永栽 打造横跨银行、航空、烟草、啤酒行业的跨国商业帝国，他始终认为自己事业能够基业长青，很大一部分源自儒道思想。

2015 年具有里程碑意义的马尼拉 APEC 峰会上，唐仪有幸与家父陈永栽会面。家父 分享 自少年时代便养成研读、背诵中华典籍的习惯。除《论语》《孙子兵法》《周易》之外，家父陈 还能够大段背诵《三字经》《唐诗 300 首》《古文观止》与《三国演义》。

2002 年， 陈永栽 受邀出席在北京举办的 首届中华儒商国际论坛 并发表主旨演讲，演讲题目为《诚信是创建发展企业之本》。 陈永栽 提出:诚信 是所有企业不可动摇的根基。即便身处严峻商业危机， 家父 坚决主张，绝不能为换取短期生存而出卖诚信。

当下 AI‑Web3‑区块链世界，遍布分布式远程团队、数字游民社群、跨境合作伙伴，以及从未线下碰面的 AI 数字分身协作者。为远距离跨境联盟构建稳固信任，已经成为全球创业者面临的最大挑战之一。

《RuDao》将 家父陈永栽 亲身践行的儒商实践，转化为可落地的现代行动指引。本书并非简单摘抄古籍，而是把中华千年创业智慧，对接数字时代竞合、远程联盟建设的现实痛点。每一位创业者、投资人、跨境联盟建设者都应当阅读本书。

— Prof. Timmy Tan 陳建榮Founder of LaIsla Magazine and SK Techno-lube Corporation - Honorary Professor, International University of Cooperative Education (iUCE) Niedersachsen, Germany - Alumni, Peking University PKU Entrepreneurs Association - Managing Partner (Philippines), Global CyberSecurity Alliance GCSA Son of Lucio Tan, the iconic Filipino-Chinese tycoon and Chairman of Philippine Airlines (PAL), Philippine National Bank (PNB), Allied Bank, and the Federation of Filipino-Chinese Chambers of Commerce and Industry 菲华商联总会、菲律宾航空、菲律宾国家银行、联盟银行董事长陈永栽之子。 资料来源: 中国新闻网 2002‑8‑22 https://www.chinanews.com.cn/2002-08-22/26/214616.html

ThomasCheung.cc 張俊勇 MH JP

Former Deputy to the National People's Congress (Hong Kong)

Supporting Statement for RuDao by Tony Tong

I have known Tony Tong (唐仪 TonyTong.cc) for more than a decade. He is a man of righteousness - one of the rare people I would genuinely call a true RuShang儒商 (Confucian entrepreneur): someone who seeks both moral integrity and practical success.

Our connection goes back to our student days in the United States. In 1988-1992, Tony studied Computer Engineering at the University of Minnesota (UMN.edu). I completed a Bachelor of Science in Computer Science, Statistics and Mathematics at the University of Wisconsin–Madison (Class of 1991), followed by a Master of Engineering in Operations Research and Industrial Engineering at Cornell University (Class of 1992).

In 2017, Tony and I co-founded the Hong Kong Blockchain Association (HKBA.cc), where I am a founding member and honorary chairman. I have seen firsthand how Tony, as the head of HKBA, has worked tirelessly to build and connect blockchain communities in Hong Kong and globally. Under his leadership, HKBA has become a platform that brings together founders, investors, developers, enterprises, academics and policymakers; promotes applied blockchain adoption, public education and regulatory dialogue; and strengthens Hong Kong's role as a bridge between China and the global Web3 ecosystem. His community-building work is rare and deserves deep appreciation.

As an angel investor and partner at InnoAngel.com, I also invested in Com2000.org / 2000x.net, a leading US-based enterprise in AI, blockchain and Bitcoin mining.

RuDao extends well beyond The Art of War. It examines long-term coopetition, durable partnerships and cross-border strategic alliances. In this age of AI, Web3 and blockchain, remote teams, distributed workers and digital nomads—and increasingly AI agents and digital twins—collaborate across continents, often without ever meeting in person. Building trust and sustaining stable long-distance cooperation across different countries, cultures and legal systems has become one of the greatest challenges for global entrepreneurs. This book offers vital intellectual guidance for building, maintaining and governing cross-border remote alliances.

At its core is YiLiHeYi义利合一 - the unity of righteousness and profit. This is the principle I hold in my own life, my investment decisions and my management of cross-border remote alliances. I recommend RuDao wholeheartedly to anyone building trust and lasting partnerships in the AI era.

我认识本书作者唐仪（TonyTong.cc）已逾十年。他为人重义，是我极少愿意称为真正"儒商RuShang"的人——既守道义，也成就事业。

我们的缘分始于美国求学时期。1988至1992年间，唐仪在明尼苏达大学（UMN.edu）攻读计算机工程；我则在威斯康星大学麦迪逊分校（University of Wisconsin–Madison）取得计算机科学、统计与数学理学士学位（1991届），其后在康奈尔大学（Cornell University）取得运筹学与工业工程工程硕士学位（1992届）。

2017年，我与唐仪共同创办香港區塊鏈協會（HKBA.cc），我是创会成员及荣誉主席。我亲眼见证唐仪作为协会负责人，如何不遗余力地凝聚香港及全球区块链社群。在他带领下，HKBA 搭建平台，连接创业者、投资人、开发者、企业、学者与监管机构，推动区块链应用落地、公众教育与监管对话，并强化香港作为中国与全球 Web3 生态之间的桥梁角色。他对社群建设的贡献，实属罕见，值得高度肯定与感谢。

作为天使投资人、英诺天使基金（InnoAngel.com）合伙人，我投资了Com2000.org / 2000x.net，这家美国头部企业布局人工智能、区块链与比特币挖矿。

《儒道RuDao》一书的格局超越《孙子兵法》。书中探讨长期竞合、持久伙伴关系与跨境战略联盟。身处AI、Web3与区块链蓬勃发展的时代，远程团队、分布式工作者、数字游民，以及AI智能体与数字分身，跨洲协作，很多伙伴终生无需线下会面。在不同国家、文化与法律体系之下建立信任、维系稳定的远距离合作，已成为全球创业者面临的最大难题之一。本书为搭建、维护与治理跨境远程联盟，提供了至关重要的思想指引。

全书根基是义利合一 YiLiHeYi 即道义与利益的统一。这也是我信守的人生准则，贯穿于日常生活、投资判断以及跨境远程伙伴联盟的管理之中。我诚挚推荐《儒道RuDao》给每一位在AI时代构建信任与长久合作的人。

— "YiLiHeYi is the foundational trust principle for cross-border remote alliances, digital nomads and Web3 builders in the AI era." -- Thomas Cheung MH JP "义利合一，是AI时代数字游民、Web3建设者搭建跨境远程联盟的信任根基。" ThomasCheung.cc 張俊勇 MH JP 香港區塊鏈協會 HKBA.cc 荣誉主席及创会成员 原港区全国人大代表 - 香港英诺天使基金 InnoAngel.com 合伙人 - HKBC Gold Limited 主席CEO

The Hon Alan Chan 陈宗彝议员

Legislative Councillor of the Hong Kong Special Administrative Region, China 中华人民共和国香港特别行政区立法会议员

本书问世之际，世界已然丧失了对和谐的胃口。冷战结束后的三十年间，直至本世纪第一个十年，全球经济以年均约3.7%的速度增长。那段时期并非没有冲突，但其底色是对共同利益的预设。各国可以各持己见，却仍能彼此通商；竞争者可以并存，而双方皆能繁荣。然而自2008年以来，增长已放缓至年均约2.7%。这块饼的扩张速度，已不足以容纳所有觊觎者的雄心。当增长放缓，"你赢我输"的逻辑便变得危险地诱人。地缘政治向零和算计倾斜。关税、制裁与出口管制，不再是最后手段，而成了第一本能。

这便是唐仪（TonyTong）《儒道-超越孙子兵法》一书所面对的世界。而这正是时机之所以关键的原因。

中华文明立于三大支柱之上:儒家、佛家与道家。三者各自提供了一套独特的世界取向。儒家入世——它涉足家庭、社会与治理的人间领域，力求通过礼教、互惠与道德修养来理顺关系。佛家出世——它超脱于尘世执著，以求通过洞见与慈悲从苦难中解脱。道家浮世——它飘然物外，顺应事物之自然流转，于不争之中觅得功效。

了解儒家，便了解了中华文明的三分之一。要把握其余三分之二，则需要唐仪在本书中所进行的那种探索。书名中的"儒"即儒家；"道"即道路——而道路并非单一之途，而是诸传统的交汇。唐仪并不假装对三家学说作完整阐述。他提供的是一种更为珍贵的东西:一座连接西方战略思维与东方关系智慧的桥梁。

西方战略传统，尤其是凝结于《孙子兵法》及其众多后学之中的那一传统，是一种优势的传统。西方会问:我如何相对于对手定位自己？我如何利用弱点、集中力量、克敌制胜？这并非一种愤世嫉俗的传统——而是一种现实主义的传统。但其逻辑倾向于零和。博弈被框定为赢家与输家、胜者与败者。在其较为粗陋的表现形态中，它造就了这样一个世界:一国的安全以另一国的不安全为代价购得，合作被疑为软弱，而国际体系宛如一张桌子——有人坐于桌旁，有人则列于菜单之上。

儒家提供了一个不同的起点。它并不否认自利，但拒绝将自利视为唯一的利益。儒家的洞见在于:人的昌盛是关系性的。个体由其种种关系所构成——父与子、君与臣、友与友。孤立地追求一己之善，便是误解了善本身究竟为何物。这正是儒家思想强调互惠的原因:"己欲立而立人，己欲达而达人"——要成就自己，便成就他人；要通达自己，便通达他人。

这并非天真的理想主义。这是一种诞生于一个存续数千年之文明的实践智慧，其秘诀恰恰在于领悟到:纯粹的支配不可持续。儒家的秩序愿景，不是赢家与输家的等级序列，而是相互义务交织成的一张网。它自身是一种战略——但有别于西方的另类战略。它寻求的不是对他人的胜利，而是与他们的和谐共赢。

全球经济如今正面临经济学家们委婉称之为"存量竞争"的局面。轻松增长的时代已经过去。人口结构已然逆转。生产率增长已然放缓。债务负担制约着财政空间。在这样一个世界里，将国际关系视为一场固定总和之博弈的诱惑变得难以抗拒。若我的邻邦增长，我邦便必定萎缩。若另一国崛起，我国便必定衰落。这正是二十世纪催生两次世界大战的逻辑。这是我们在二十一世纪承受不起的逻辑。

正是在这里，儒家智慧提供了西方太过经常忽视的某种东西:一种理解框架——互惠不是施舍，合作不是投降。儒家的"王道"概念——"以武服人者霸，以德服人者王"——以武力使人屈服者是霸者，以德行赢得人心者才是真正的王者——王道不在于支配他人，而在于赢得人心。这是一种通过信任与正当性、而非通过胁迫与恐惧来运作的力量形式。就最实际的意义而言，它是一种更可持续的影响力形态。

唐仪 TonyTong 居于独一无二的位置，足以在这两个世界之间架起桥梁。他受过工程师的训练，以科技创业为实践，以战略研习为性情，数十年来穿梭于东西方相遇的种种空间。他的职业生涯横跨美国与香港、科技与金融、董事会与课堂。他久居西方而通晓西方的战略思维。他从养育他的土壤中通晓儒家的传统。《儒道》不是一部学术论著。它是一位实践者对于东方能教给一个已厌倦输赢的世界些什么的反思。

这不是一本许诺简易答案的书。它并不声称儒家将解决地缘政治，或恢复增长率。它所提供的是一种思维方式——一种在他人看到交易之处看到关系、在他人看到短期利益之处看到长期和谐的思维方式。在一个存量竞争与零和诱惑的时代，这种思维方式不是奢侈品。它是必需品。

带着对一部东方战略手册的期待翻开本书的读者，将会发现另一些东西:一场关于战略究竟为何而存在的沉思。唐仪不问如何取胜。他问取胜意味着什么。而在这样做时，他邀请我们思考:最持久的胜利，不是那些战胜他人的胜利，而是那些与他人共同赢得的胜利。

This book arrives at a time when the world has lost its appetite for harmony.

For three decades, from the end of the Cold War through the first decade of this century, the global economy expanded at an average of roughly 3.7 percent annually. That period was not without conflict, but it was animated by a presumption of shared gains. Nations could disagree and still trade. Competitors could coexist and both prosper. Since 2008, however, growth has decelerated to an average of approximately 2.7 percent. The pie is no longer expanding fast enough to absorb the ambitions of all who seek a slice. When growth slows, the logic of "you win, I lose" becomes dangerously seductive. Geopolitics bends toward zero-sum calculation. Tariffs, sanctions, and export controls emerge not as instruments of last resort but as tools of first instinct.

This is the world into which Tony Tong's RuDao — Beyond the Art of War speaks. And it is precisely why the timing matters.

Chinese civilization rests on three pillars: Confucianism, Buddhism, and Daoism. Each offers a distinct orientation toward the world. Confucianism is engaged — it wades into the human realm of family, society, and governance, seeking to order relationships through ritual, reciprocity, and moral cultivation. Buddhism is transcendent — it withdraws from worldly attachment, seeking liberation from suffering through insight and compassion. Daoism is elusive — it floats, yielding to the natural flow of things, finding efficacy in non-contention.

To understand Confucianism is to understand one-third of Chinese civilization. To grasp the other two-thirds requires the kind of exploration that Tony Tong undertakes in these pages. The "Ru" of the title is Confucianism; the "Dao" is the Way — and the way is not a single path but a confluence of traditions. Tony Tong does not pretend to offer a complete exposition of all three schools. He offers something more valuable: a bridge between the strategic mind of the West and the relational wisdom of the East.

The Western strategic tradition, particularly as crystallized in The Art of War and its many progeny, is a tradition of advantage. It asks: How do I position myself relative to my adversary? How do I exploit weakness, concentrate force, and prevail? This is not a cynical tradition — it is a realist one. But its logic tends toward the zero-sum. The game is framed as winner and loser, victor and vanquished. In its cruder manifestations, it has produced a world where one nation's security is purchased with another's insecurity, where cooperation is mistrusted as weakness, and where the international system resembles a table at which some sit and others are on the menu.

Confucianism offers a different starting point. It does not deny self-interest, but it refuses to treat self-interest as the only interest. The Confucian insight is that human flourishing is relational. The individual is constituted by their relationships — parent and child, ruler and minister, friend and friend. To pursue one's own good in isolation is to misunderstand what the good actually is. This is why Confucian thought emphasizes mutuality: "己欲立而立人，己欲达而达人" — to establish oneself, one establishes others; to achieve oneself, one achieves others.

This is not naive idealism. It is a practical wisdom born of a civilization that has survived millennia precisely by learning that pure domination is unsustainable. The Confucian vision of order is not a hierarchy of winners and losers but a web of reciprocal obligations. It is, in its own way, a strategy — but a strategy of a different kind. It seeks not victory over others but harmony with them.

The global economy now faces what economists politely call "stock competition." The era of easy growth is behind us. Demographics have turned. Productivity gains have slowed. Debt burdens constrain fiscal space. In such a world, the temptation to see international relations as a fixed-sum game becomes overwhelming. If my neighbor grows, I must be shrinking. If another nation rises, I must be declining. This is the logic that produced two world wars in the twentieth century. It is a logic we cannot afford in the twenty-first.

This is where Confucian wisdom offers something the West has too often ignored: a framework for understanding that mutuality is not charity, and cooperation is not capitulation. The Confucian concept of wang dao (王道) — "以武服人者霸，以德服人者王" — he who subdues others by force is a hegemon; he who wins others by virtue is a true king — the kingly way is not about dominating others but about winning their hearts. It is a form of power that operates through trust and legitimacy rather than coercion and fear. It is, in the most practical sense, a more sustainable form of influence.

Tony Tong is uniquely positioned to bridge these worlds. An engineer by training, a technology entrepreneur by practice, a student of strategy by temperament, he has spent decades navigating the spaces where East and West meet. His career has spanned the United States and Hong Kong, technology and finance, the boardroom and the classroom. He knows the Western strategic mind from the inside. He knows the Confucian tradition from the soil that raised him. RuDao is not an academic treatise. It is a practitioner's reflection on what the East can teach a world that has grown tired of winning and losing.

This is not a book that promises easy answers. It does not claim that Confucianism will solve geopolitics or restore growth rates. What it offers is a way of thinking — a way of seeing relationships where others see transactions, of seeing long-term harmony where others see short-term advantage. In an era of stock competition and zero-sum temptation, that way of thinking is not a luxury. It is a necessity.

The reader who comes to this book expecting a manual of Eastern strategy will find something else: a meditation on what strategy is for. Tony Tong does not ask how to win. He asks what winning means. And in doing so, he invites us to consider that the most enduring victories are not those won over others, but those won with them.

— The Hon Alan Chan 陈宗彝议员 · Legislative Councillor of the Hong Kong Special Administrative Region, China 中华人民共和国香港特别行政区立法会议员

Yvonne Wang 王瑜琨

CEO, JinSe 金色财经 · Web3 Media

RuDao.org 推荐语:TonyTong.cc 唐仪先生的这本《儒道RuDao》，总是让我想起马克斯·韦伯 Max Weber 的《新教伦理和资本主义精神 The Protestant Ethic and the Spirit of Capitalism》。如果说马克斯·韦伯在《新教伦理与资本主义精神》中为西方奠定了资本主义新教伦理，那么唐仪先生这本《儒道RuDao》，或许可以视为一次从东方思想资源出发的、面向数字时代的"伦理重构"尝试。唐仪先生以区块链行业的多年实践为底色，回望中国儒道传统，其用意或许正是在于，当技术赋予个体前所未有的主权时，我们更需要一种内在的伦理自觉来驾驭这种自由。《儒道》值得每一位关注 Web3 和 AI 时代精神的人认真对待。

— Yvonne Wang 王瑜琨 · CEO 金色财经 JinSe2.com

Reference · 参考文献

马克斯·韦伯 Max Weber — 《新教伦理与资本主义精神》The Protestant Ethic and the Spirit of Capitalism（1904–1905）

社会学与经济史的经典名著，探讨宗教观念如何直接影响经济行为和资本主义的发展。

  • The Spirit of Capitalism: Weber defines this not as mere greed or the desire for wealth, but as a unique ethos — a systematic, rational pursuit of economic gain combined with self-denial and strict discipline. Work is viewed not just as a way to survive, but as a moral duty.
  • 资本主义精神:韦伯所指的"资本主义精神"，不是单纯对财富的贪婪，而是一种追求合理化、系统化、节制且持续地赚取利润的生活态度与伦理观（如本杰明·富兰克林所体现的勤勉、守时、视工作为天职的精神）。
  • The Concept of the "Calling" (Beruf): Introduced by Martin Luther, this idea suggested that secular, everyday work was a religious duty and a way to serve God. It broke down the old Catholic hierarchy that favored the monk or priest over the merchant or craftsman.
  • 天职观 Beruf:新教打破了天主教将"神圣生活"与"世俗工作"分开的观念，认为每个人在世俗岗位上的勤勉工作都是对上帝的召唤，是履行天职。
  • 入世苦行 Asceticism: Achieving economic success through hard work and self-discipline was seen as a sign of salvation. Because their religion strictly forbade spending money on luxury or personal pleasure, they reinvested their profits back into their businesses, effectively creating modern capitalism.
  • 新教伦理的两面:新教徒一方面倡导极度勤奋、克制消费、反对享乐；另一方面又把通过正当劳动积累财富视为上帝恩典的体现。
  • 历史的衍生结果:这种"拼命赚钱却不纵情享乐"的入世禁欲主义，直接催生了资本原始积累所需的资本、自由劳动力与理性化经营方式，最终在不知不觉中构建了现代资本主义的社会结构。

Dr. Christian Wei 魏学纲博士

Founder & President, EUCON International School and University 美国尤肯国际学校及大学创始人及校长 · Senior Pastor, Christian Bible Church, Saipan, USA 美国塞班基督徒圣经教会主任牧师 · 美国教育部 TRACS 高校鉴定委员会委员、鉴定专家

TonyTong's new book RuDao — Beyond the Art of War《儒道-超越孙子兵法》评鉴 Reader's Review by Dr. Christian Wei, Pastor, Christian Bible Church, Saipan, USA

一份以传统中国伦理为参照、以基要派基督教信仰为立场的读后评述。

说明:本评鉴依据的材料为三篇推荐序、献言、第一章开篇、作者简介、框架图表（五常、三大原则、孙子兵法对照表、思想谱系）。

一、总评

《儒道》要做的事不小:把儒家的义利之辨、道家的天人观，以及近年中国商界讨论的"儒商""儒将"传统，整合成一套可操作的现代商业"竞合"框架，并与《孙子兵法》对话。作者唐仪有多年跨境创业、投资和区块链行业的实务经验，书中的问题意识也比较具体:在远程协作、跨国联盟、AI 与 Web3 的环境里，陌生人之间如何建立长期信任？

这个问题选得好，也切合时代。就现有材料看，本书的价值在实践性的伦理提醒与文化普及，不在严格的学术论证。以下分三部分评述:值得肯定之处，学理上可商榷之处，以及从基督教信仰立场出发的回应。

二、值得肯定之处

1. 以"信"为本，直指商业的根基。书中序言反复强调诚信，引述陈建荣之父陈永栽（Lucio Tan）2002 年"诚信是企业立业之本"的演讲，主张即使在危机中也不以诚信换取短期生存。这是对商业伦理核心的正确把握。无论哪种文化，长期合作都建立在可预期的守信之上。

2. 不把利润妖魔化。作者提出"利润不是原则的敌人，贪婪才是"，区分了正当之利与贪婪。这与儒家"君子爱财，取之有道"、司马迁《货殖列传》对正当经商的肯定一脉相承。书中以《史记》开篇，也说明作者不回避人性趋利的现实（"天下熙熙，皆为利来"），然后再提出规范，这个写法是诚实的。

3. 对《孙子兵法》的处理态度较为克制。作者明言"不是拒绝，而是吸收"，承认兵法对"零和层面"仍然有效，这比简单批判更成熟。"并非每场相遇都是战争"这一追问本身是有力的。

4. 孝道与师承的底色。献言部分对父母、师长、母校校训的感念（慎思笃行），表现出真实的感恩与教养，不是空泛的口号。

5. 推荐序提供的人证。叶匡时教授从学术与公共行政角度、张俊勇和陈建荣从商界与家族实践角度作序，使本书带有"实践见证"的色彩。

三、学理上可商榷之处

以下是从学术角度必须指出的问题，也是评价一本书是否经得起推敲的关键。

1. 对《孙子兵法》的概括偏简化。对照表把兵法归为"零和、欺骗、功利不论道德"，作营销图表可以，作学术判断则不够公允。《孙子》本身强调"慎战"（"兵者，国之大事，死生之地"），主张"上兵伐谋"和"不战而屈人之兵"，也重视"道"（令民与上同意）与"将"的"智信仁勇严"。兵法并非完全"无道德"，这一点读者应参照原典自行判断。

2. 概念之间的界线有些混用。"义利合一"与"先义后利"在思想史上不是一回事:前者更接近宋明以后（及近现代）的表述，后者出自《荀子》等传统。陈建荣序言中把 "Righteousness before Profit" 与"义利合一"并置，有概念混淆之嫌。全书同时出现"八德"（第二章）与"五常"（图表），二者是不同的伦理系统（五常为仁义礼智信，八德通常为忠孝仁爱信义和平），读者需要作者说明二者的关系。"天人合一"在书中被归为道家，而这一概念在儒家（如张载、董仲舒一系）同样重要，学界对其内涵也有不同解释。

3. "以德为略"的内在张力。把美德当作战略，容易让美德成为手段。儒家本来强调"为己之学"与"义以为上"，若德行只因"划算"才被实践，则当德行与利益冲突时，这套框架的说服力将大打折扣。书中说"设计道德之路同时也是获利之路的交易"，这在理想情况下成立，但现实中常有两者冲突之时，孔子所谓"杀身成仁""舍生取义"恰恰指向这种时刻。希望正文对此有更诚实的处理。

4. 实证依据尚待检验。"三十年跨境商业经验证实了……"这类说法，若无案例、数据和反例，只能算经验见证，不能算论证。

5. 商业推广与学术著作的界线。材料中的作者简介、演讲名单、预购页面等明显具有营销性质。这本身无可厚非，但读者应区分"宣传材料"与"书的实际内容"。

四、从基督教基要主义立场的回应 Christian Review Angle

我尽量公平地说:基要派信仰有其不可让步的核心（圣经无误、神的位格与创造、人的堕落、因信称义、基督的独一性），评论一本非基督教著作时，既不必苛责它"不是基督教"，也不能含糊地说"殊途同归"。

（一）可以欣然承认的共同点（普遍恩典）

神的普遍恩典使不信者也可能认识并实践部分道德真理（罗马书 2:14-15，良心的见证）。书中许多主张与圣经的智慧文学和教导相合:

诚信与公平交易:箴言 11:1、20:10，"诡诈的天平为耶和华所憎恶"，与书中"信"的主张高度一致。

化敌为友:罗马书 12:18-21、马太福音 5:44，爱仇敌、"尽可能与众人和睦"，与"竞合并行、化敌为友"在方向上相近。

拒绝不义之财:箴言 16:8，"少有财物，行事公义，强如多有财利，行事不义"，近于"君子见义勇为、不义而富且贵于我如浮云"。

孝道:出埃及记 20:12、以弗所书 6:2，圣经同样重视孝敬父母，作者的献言可以被基督徒真诚地欣赏。

劳动与职业的尊严:歌罗西书 3:23，"无论做什么，都要从心里做，像是给主做的"，基督教并不轻看商业。

（二）需要明确保留与分辨之处

1. 人性论的根本差异。儒家传统（尤其孟子）倾向"性善"，并相信通过修身可以成德；圣经则教导人在亚当里整体堕落（罗马书 3:10-23，"没有义人，连一个也没有"）。因此书中"修身—成德—成功"的路径，在基督徒看来缺少了人无法靠自己成义这一前提，也缺少了恩典的维度（以弗所书 2:8-9）。美德能改善行为与关系，却不能解决罪与审判的问题。

2. "以德为略"的危险，圣经有更尖锐的话。耶稣问:"人若赚得全世界，赔上自己的生命，有什么益处呢？"（马可福音 8:36）。又说:"你们不能又侍奉神，又侍奉玛门。"（马太福音 6:24）基督徒的动机不是"德行带来长久利润"，而是"为了荣耀神并爱邻舍"。若德行以回报为前提，圣经称之为"外表"的义（马太福音 6:1-2，在人面前行善）。书中"义利合一"在实务层面有可取之处，但基督徒不能把"利"放在与"义"并列的终极位置，提摩太前书 6:10 也提醒"贪财是万恶之根"。

3. "天人合一"与创造者—被造者之别。圣经强调神是位格的、超越的创造者，人与天地万物是被造的，与神"有分别"而非"合一"。若"天人合一"被理解为人与宇宙的和谐，这可以与"神的创造秩序"和"管理大地"（创世记 1:28）对话；若被理解为天、地、人同属一个自足的生命整体，或把"道"当作终极实在，则与基督教有位格神的教义根本不同。基要派读者应在此划清界线。

4. 阴阳符号与宗教混合的顾虑。封面、海报等采用阴阳图样、"和而不同"等语汇，基督徒读者应意识到其中的宗教哲学意涵，在个人信仰与教会生活中保持分辨（哥林多后书 6:14-17）。这并不等于不能阅读或欣赏，而是阅读时需要有清晰的立场。

5. 孔子与"礼"。儒家的"礼"含有祭祖等宗教仪式成分。就现有材料看，书中主要从"礼的形式与信任的建构"角度谈礼，未见涉及祭祀问题，这点无可指责，但基督徒读者在实践中仍需分辨。

6. 成功观与"繁荣"叙事。书中有不少与创业、投资、区块链行业相关的内容与荣誉叙述。基督徒应以雅各书 4:13-16 的提醒看待一切商业计划:"你们只当说:主若愿意……"，不把成功当作义的凭证，也不把财富当作蒙福的标志。

五、综合评价与推荐

优点:问题意识清晰；主张诚信、互利、长期主义；以中国传统资源回应现代跨境协作；语言通达，适合商界读者；个人见证真实。

不足:对兵法的概括过于简化；若干传统概念界线需更精确；"以德为略"的动机张力有待处理；实证与案例支撑需在正文中检验；营销色彩较重。

适合谁读:对中国商业伦理、儒商传统感兴趣的创业者与职场人士；从事跨国、跨文化合作的人；基督徒商人与学者——可作为与华人商界对话的入门材料，借此了解对方的价值语言。

阅读建议（给基督徒读者）:把书中的"信、义、仁、孝"视为普遍恩典下的道德见证，欣赏而不神化；遇到"天人合一""以德为略"之处，用圣经作准绳，分辨而不全盘接受或否定；对照阅读《论语》《孟子》《孙子兵法》原典，以及圣经的箴言、雅各书、马太福音 5 至 7 章，比较其异同；把本书当作"福音预工"的对话桥梁，而不是"另一条救恩之路"。

Endorsement 推荐语（可用于书评或推荐序）:《儒道》以"信"与"义"为轴，提醒商业世界:长远的合作靠的是可靠的人，而非高明的手段。书中对化敌为友、拒绝不义之财的主张，与历代圣贤乃至圣经的智慧相互呼应。惟盼读者在欣赏之余，亦能追问:德行的根基何在？人若无法靠自己成义，美德之外还需要什么？这部书不是答案的终点，却是一个值得认真对话的起点。

Rating 总评:4/5 ★★★★☆（实用性与问题意识佳；学理严谨度与神学根基有待补强。基督徒读者宜"取其善、辨其异、以经为准"。）

— 魏学纲博士 Dr. Christian Wei · 美国尤肯国际学校及大学 (EUCON International School and University) 创始人及校长 · 美国塞班基督徒圣经教会 (Christian Bible Church) 主任牧师 · 美国教育部 TRACS 高校鉴定委员会委员、鉴定专家 · eucon.us/en/about-us/about-the-president

About the Endorser · 推荐人简介

Ander Tsui

Co-Chairman, HKBA.cc 香港区块链协会共同主席 · Founder, Vertex.Capital London UK

RuDao.org Endorsement 推荐语:在技术重构全球协作版图的今天，AI 与分布式网络让全球创业者、远程团队及数字游民紧密互联，但跨越地域、文化与制度的信任难题亦前所未有。唐仪（Tony Tong）先生的新作《儒道》（RuDao），以极具前瞻性的战略视野，突破了传统零和博弈与《孙子兵法》式计谋的局限，提出了面向智能时代跨境生态的竞合之道。

全书精髓所系，正是东方智慧中的"义利合一"。唐仪先生将数十年的工程视野、全球跨境商业实战与现代科技治理相融合，为 AI Agent、分布式组织及跨国战略联盟的长期信任搭建了坚实的心智基座。这不仅是一本献给全球创业者与投资人的治理指南，更是一部在算法时代重塑商业契约精神的哲思之作。

— Ander Tsui · 香港区块链协会 HKBA.cc 共同主席 · Web3 Investor Vertex.Capital 创始人

Glory Gao 高雷

深圳国学院运营负责人 Shenzhen Academy of Chinese Studies

RuDao.org book review: 我经营国学综合平台这些年，接触过不少修行人。

很多人会说自己"佛道双修"，但极少有人说自己"儒道双修"——更少的，是真正修出结果来的人。

唐会长，是我目前接触到的唯一一位提出"儒道"的老师。

他不是只在书斋里讲道理。无论是在筹建区块链协会的过程中，还是在日常待人接物的分寸里，他的做法和书里写的那一套，是同一套——说到，也做到。

这套心法，也不是凭空来的。是他走过各行各业，见过足够多的人、做过足够多的事之后，一点一点反刍、总结出来的。所以读起来不飘，每一句都能落到具体的处境上。

我一直有个判断:

如果说《孙子兵法》适合各类博弈的商战，那么《儒道》则是适用于新时代发展下的共生、共建、共赢的商业合作关系。

《孙子兵法 Art of War》解决的是"如何在博弈中赢"，
《儒道 RuDao》解决的是"如何让人愿意和你一起赢"。

而今天，我们比任何时候都需要后者。

仁义礼智信，被太多人抛诸脑后；
无为而治，被误读成躺平；
道义，被当成了不合时宜的旧词。

于是我们看到:社会的信任体系正在面临崩塌的危机——权责利的混乱、债务的逾期、人与人之间的种种不信任，都在给社会的进步按下倒退键。

所有的逾期，本质上都是信义的逾期；
所有的内耗，本质上都是信任的透支。

而对我个人而言，遇到唐会长是一件很幸运的事——他替我解开了压在心里很久的一个困惑。

自我有独立思想起，我一直信奉儒家。可真正进入社会才发现，越是照着儒家那一套去做人做事，越容易碰壁:被打击，被算计，一次次遇上背信弃义的人。

久而久之，我只能把"儒"藏起来，改用道家的方式去处理问题、保护自己。说好听些是通透，说实在些，是失望之后的退守。

直到唐会长点醒我:原来儒与道，本就可以合在一起。儒是底色，道是方法——内有仁义，外有方圆；你不必在"做个好人"和"保护好自己"之间二选一。

所以我是真诚地把 TonyTong《儒道 RuDao》这本书推荐给大家。

希望你能和我一样，读完这本书，有所思、有所感、有所悟；
希望你能找到自己一直前行的方向，以及坚持正确方向的决心；
也希望我们的社会越来越好，人与人之间重新讲良心、讲道义；
更希望我们这一代人，能把仁义礼智信重新立起来——让"相信一个人"重新变成一件不需要勇气的事，让越来越多的人愿意相信:讲道义，是有价值的。

一本比《孙子兵法》更适用于现代社会的《儒道 RuDao》，值得你拥有。

-- Glory Gao 高雷 · 深圳国学院运营负责人 Shenzhen Academy of Chinese Studies

About · 关于美中英才教育基金会

美中英才教育基金会（US-China YingCai Education Foundation）是经美国联邦政府审核批准的教育基金会，致力于推动中美青少年文化教育及艺术交流，并与两国政府部门、教育局、80 余所美国高中及 100 余所大学建立合作关系，覆盖美国 50 个州。其教育体系涵盖幼儿园至大学全阶段，结合区域文化设计课程，并设立困难学生奖学金、优秀学生全额奖学金及单项突出部分奖学金。

Jason Guo

Founder, StarSpace Capital 创始人

推荐语 for TonyTong.cc:做早期投资这些年，我见过太多项目不是输在技术和商业模式，就是输在团队散了。唐仪 TonyTong 先生的新作《儒道 RuDao》把话说开了:孙子教人怎么赢对手，他问的是赢完之后关系还在不在。

《儒道》没把儒家思想拿来讲修养，而是拿来当生意的规矩:该争的地方争，该一起把盘子做大的地方别内讧，利润和信任不是二选一。AI 这波，工具会更强，但信任只会更贵。本书从兵法里拉出来，写成能用的框架，比任何一本商战鸡汤有用。

— Jason Guo · Founder, StarSpace Capital 创始人

Kong Yanqing 孔彦卿

Council Member, Confucius Culture Inheritance Working Committee · 74th-generation descendant of Confucius

Business tests a person's integrity when profit is at stake and reveals the breadth of their regard for others. Confucianism teaches us to "consider what is right when faced with gain," while Taoism teaches us to "follow the natural order." These teachings encourage business leaders to consider others as they pursue profit, respect the underlying order of things, discern when to advance or step back, and exercise restraint amid change. As a descendant of Confucius, I have always believed that profound business wisdom lies in remaining faithful to one's principles and commitments as interests and circumstances change, preserving the possibility of lasting growth for all involved.

Drawing on years of international business experience, Tony Tong explores in Ru-Tao the balance between the pursuit of profit and enduring partnerships. The value of this inquiry lies in bringing benevolence, righteousness, and trust into concrete business relationships, while taking conflicting interests and competitive pressures seriously. The difficulties of cooperation often emerge when interests change: whether promises can be kept, how gains should be distributed, and whether partners retain the opportunity to continue working together. It is through these choices that Confucian and Taoist wisdom acquires practical significance. I recommend this book to every business leader who values integrity and takes the long view. Lasting business relationships ultimately depend on earning one another's trust through action when interests change.

商业最考验一个人在利益面前的操守，也最照见其对待他人的格局。儒家讲"见利思义"，道家讲"道法自然"，启发经营者在追求利润时顾及他人，在变化之中尊重规律、知进退、守分寸。身为孔子后裔，我始终认为，商业智慧的深处，在于利害变幻之际，仍能守住信义，为彼此留下长久的生机。

唐仪 TonyTong 先生以多年跨国商业实践为基础，在《儒道RuDao》中探讨利润追求与长久伙伴关系之间的平衡。这一思考的可贵之处，在于将仁义与信任放回具体的商业关系之中，认真面对利益分歧与竞争压力。合作的难处，往往在利益发生变化之后:能否兑现承诺，如何分配所得，又能否为对方保留继续合作的空间。儒道智慧在这些选择中，才显出切实的分量。我愿将此书推荐给每一位看重信义、着眼长久的经营者。商业的长久，终究要在利益变化之后，以行动赢得彼此的信任。

— Kong Yanqing - Council Member, Confucius Culture Inheritance Working Committee, China Traditional Culture Promotion Association - 74th-generation descendant of Confucius 孔彦卿 - 中国传统文化促进会孔子文化传承工作委员会理事、孔子第74代孙

Eddy Chi Ho Ko 高志豪 香港 英皇書院同學會 King's College KCOBA.org Kings.edu.hk

Hong Kong · King's College Old Boys Alumni (KCOBA) · Ex-Greater China Director of Labuan IBFC, Malaysia

Endorsement for RuDao · Beyond The Art of War by Tony Tong

As someone who has navigated the complexities of business and leadership in Hong Kong for years, I have seen countless books on strategy, negotiation, and competition. Yet few have truly captured the cultural and philosophical depth required to build lasting success in today's interconnected world. Tony Tong's RuDao - Beyond The Art of War does exactly that—and more.

This ebook is not merely another take on Sun Tzu's Art of War. It boldly moves beyond the classic win-lose paradigm and offers a refreshing, Confucian-inspired framework of co-opetition—where profit and partnership coexist, and where long-term relationships outweigh short-term victories. Tony eloquently bridges ancient Chinese wisdom with modern business realities, showing readers how to balance commercial ambition with ethical responsibility and mutual respect.

What resonates most with me is the book's practical relevance. Whether you are a startup founder, a corporate executive, or a negotiator, RuDao provides actionable insights on how to cultivate trust, manage conflict, and create value that endures. In a world often obsessed with disruption and domination, Tony reminds us that true strategic mastery lies in harmony, not conquest.

I highly recommend RuDao - Beyond The Art of War to anyone seeking a more sustainable, humane, and ultimately more successful approach to business and life. Tony Tong has written a timely and transformative guide!

— Eddy Chi Ho Ko, Hong Kong, King's College Old Boys Alumni KCOBA Ex-Greater China Director of Labuan IBFC of Malaysia Government

Li RunMin 李润铭

HKBA Beijing · 题:以儒立身 以道养心

"儒道 RuDao"二字，不仅是书名，更是中国人数千年来的精神底色。在这本书里，我的朋友没有做枯燥的学理考据，而是将儒家的进取与道家的超脱化作了笔下的烟火与山河。读这本书，像是在喧嚣尘世中寻得一处清凉地——既有"知其不可而为之"的执着，又有"乘天地之正"的逍遥。如果你也在入世与出世之间徘徊，这本书会给你最好的答案。

Mark Xiao XinHua 肖新華

Toronto

读《儒道 RuDao 》有感

多伦多的秋夜总是来得很早，窗外枫叶在路灯下泛着微红，凉意顺着窗缝渗进书房。我泡了一壶陈年普洱，翻开唐仪先生的《RuDao儒道-超越孙子兵法》，原本只想在睡前随手翻阅几页，却不知不觉读到了深夜。合上书本时，茶已微凉，心中却涌起一股久违的温热。在这个充满不确定性的时代，能遇到这样一本既有文化厚度、又有实战温度的书，实属难得。

诚于书:从书斋到战场的跨越

读唐仪先生的书，最先打动我的是他的"诚"。这不是那种四平八稳的学术腔调，而是一个在商海沉浮数十年的实战者，把真金白银换来的教训与智慧，毫无保留地摊开在读者面前。

全书分为内圣篇、外王篇、融合篇三大部分，结构严谨却不刻板。唐先生提出的"儒将竞合""义利共存""竞合并行""共赢联盟"等核心概念，不是凭空捏造的理论，而是他从Web 1.0到Web 4.0的跨界实战中提炼出的生存法则。他生于桂林，十岁来港，十八岁赴美，1986年从香港大学毕业，2000年联合创立纳斯达克上市企业Pacificnet，持有美国互联网工作流技术专利，又曾在高盛、摩根大通、瑞银等顶级金融机构的峰会上演讲。这样的履历，让他笔下的每一个字都带着泥土和硝烟的气息。

他谈AI治理、Web3，不是赶时髦，而是真正思考过这些前沿技术如何与儒家智慧结合。他强调"内圣外王"——儒商与儒将必须是同一个人，这话说得斩钉截铁，没有半点含糊。读这样的书，你不会觉得作者在居高临下地教导你，而是像一位老友在炉火旁，把半生心得娓娓道来。

诚于心:利润与道义并非不可兼得

书中有一句话让我反复咀嚼:"义以生利"。这是子贡的智慧，也是唐先生一生的信条。在商学院里，我们被教导利润最大化是企业的天职，道义往往被当作锦上添花的装饰，甚至被视为成本的负担。但唐先生用他的经历告诉我，这两者并非不可兼得，关键在于"诚"。

信，是不贬值的资产。在区块链的世界里，共识机制靠的是代码和算力；但在人的世界里，共识靠的是"诚"。唐先生作为香港区块链协会的联合创始人，比谁都清楚技术的边界，但他更清楚，技术再先进，也替代不了人与人之间那份沉甸甸的信任。

我曾在多伦多经历过几次商业谈判，对方精明、专业，但眼神里总有一丝戒备。后来我试着放下套路，把底牌亮出来，把困难和诚意都说清楚，反而促成了合作。这不是天真，而是明白了"诚"才是最高效的沟通方式。唐先生在书中引用儒家经典，不是复古，而是在提醒我们:在算法越来越聪明的时代，人之所以为人，就在于那份不计较短期得失的"诚"。

诚于行:知行合一在多元土壤中的生根

多伦多是一座移民城市，商业环境多元而复杂。在这里，你每天都会遇到不同文化背景、不同价值观的人。初来乍到时，我曾试图用一套放之四海而皆准的"西方商业逻辑"去应对一切，结果屡屡碰壁。

读完《儒道》，我才意识到，真正的"外王"，不是把一种模式强加于人，而是在尊重差异的基础上，找到"竞合并行"的平衡点。唐先生说"儒将竞合"，这个"竞"字用得极妙。竞争不是零和博弈，而是在竞争中彼此成就；合作也不是无原则的妥协，而是在共同目标下的相互砥砺。

在多伦多，我见过太多企业因为文化冲突而分崩离析，也见过少数族裔企业因为坚守"诚"而赢得主流市场的尊重。唐先生的书让我更加确信:知行合一，不是把书本上的道理生搬硬套到现实中，而是在每一次选择、每一次谈判、每一次危机处理中，都问问自己:我的心，诚不诚？

诚致远:写给这个时代商业人的一封信

如果让我用一句话总结这本书，我会说:这是一封写给这个时代商业人的信。

我们正处在一个"超越孙子兵法"的时代。孙子讲"兵者，诡道也"，那是冷兵器时代的生存智慧；但在今天这个万物互联、信息透明的世界里，"诡道"的成本越来越高，而"诚"的收益越来越大。唐先生提出的"共赢联盟"，不是乌托邦式的幻想，而是对现实最清醒的回应。化敌为友，不是软弱，而是格局；共赢联盟，不是施舍，而是远见。

我常想，如果更多商业领袖能读一读这本书，或许我们的商业世界会少一些尔虞我诈，多一些彼此成就。唐先生没有给我们一套万能公式，但他给了我们一颗"诚"心。有了这颗心，无论技术如何迭代，市场如何变幻，我们都能找到属于自己的"儒道"。

合上书，窗外的枫叶在风中轻轻摇曳。多伦多的秋夜依旧微凉，但我的心里，却像被一盏灯照亮了。诚于书，诚于心，诚于行，诚致远——这八个字，不仅是唐仪先生的《儒道》，也是我想对自己、对这个时代说的话。

— 肖新华 2026年10月3日 于多伦多

Geoffrey Wang 王西华

第一和谐投资有限公司董事长、天使投资人

商业世界长久推崇《孙子兵法》，讲究博弈取胜、克敌制胜。但历经数十年跨境投资与创业实践，我深深体会:短期的博弈胜利易得，长久可信的伙伴关系难建。

唐仪（Tony Tong）先生新作《儒道 RuDao 超越孙子兵法》，跳出零和博弈的商战思维，把儒家"义以生利"的古老智慧转化为数字化时代可落地的儒将竞合体系。书中不否定兵家谋略，而是更进一步告诉我们:竞争之外尚有合作，击败对手不如化对手为盟友；利润与道义并非二选一，义利合一、竞合并行，方能构建可持续的共赢联盟。

这本书打通内圣修养与外王战略，将中华传统智慧对接AI、Web3等前沿商业场景，为企业家、投资人、跨境从业者提供一套兼顾利润、信任与长远价值的商业哲学。

非常推荐每一位看重长期主义、重视伙伴生态的商业领导者细读。

祝贺《儒道 RuDao》电子书、有声书于亚马逊全球上线！

Sarah Li

CEO and founder of BlackpearlChain.io, Silicon Valley, USA

"After three decades in Silicon Valley engineering, I've come to believe that every technology — from flash memory to distributed systems to blockchain — ultimately comes down to trust.

RuDao draws on 2,500 years of Confucian thought to ask how trust can anchor commerce in the age of AI and Web3, offering readers East and West an alternative to zero-sum competition."

「在硅谷从事工程三十年，我越来越相信:无论是芯片、分布式系统还是区块链，技术最终要解决的都是一个"信"字。

《儒道》从两千五百年的儒家思想出发，探讨在 AI 与 Web3 时代如何以信立商、化敌为友，为东西方读者提供了一种超越零和竞争的思考框架。」

— 恭喜《儒道 RuDao》出版！ Sarah Li, CEO and founder of BlackpearlChain.io

Amy Li 李娅

小奥卓越量化(珠海横琴)科技有限公司 XiaoAo.HK

我诚挚推荐《儒道 RuDao》这部杰出作品。唐仪 TonyTong 先生将数十年跨境商业与前沿技术经验融入书中，构建出一套连通古今的商业哲学。本书深入剖析了如何在多变的市场中建立持久价值，兼顾财务目标与人文关怀，为各领域的决策者提供了极具实操性的指导。这是一部值得反复品读的智慧之作。

I sincerely recommend this outstanding work, RuDao. Mr. Tony Tong integrates decades of cross-border business and cutting-edge technology experience into the book, constructing a business philosophy that connects ancient and modern wisdom. It deeply explores how to build lasting value in volatile markets while balancing financial goals with humanistic care, offering highly practical guidance for decision-makers in all fields. This is a work of wisdom worthy of repeated reading.

— Amy Li 李娅 · 小奥卓越量化(珠海横琴)科技有限公司 XiaoAo.HK

Alma 木木

TECHUB News 创始人 · 香港区块链协会 HKBA 副会长/秘书长

我是 Alma 木木，TECHUB News 创始人，现任香港区块链协会副会长/秘书长。长期以来，我以香港为主要工作基地，从事科技媒体、行业交流、品牌传播与跨境合作，关注人工智能AI、区块链、Web3、金融科技及数字资产基础设施的发展。我希望通过内容、活动与社群，连接创业者、投资人、技术人才和产业机构，让有价值的创新被理解，也让志同道合的人找到彼此。

我与唐仪先生（Tony Tong）于2017年相识。对外，我称他为唐会长；在日常交往中，我更习惯称他为 Tony 哥。从相识到共同建设香港区块链协会，我们不仅分享对科技创新与行业发展的思考，也一起将这些想法落实到行业交流、社群建设与跨境合作之中。

这些年来，Tony 哥给我留下最深印象的，不只是他的创业经历和国际视野，更是他的谦逊、真诚与合作精神。他愿意倾听不同的意见，乐于分享经验，也愿意为年轻创业者和行业伙伴创造连接的机会。在我看来，他所看重的并非一次合作能带来多少短期回报，而是彼此能否建立信任、共同成长，并形成可以持续的关系。

从协会秘书长到副会长，我对行业平台的理解也在不断深化。共建香港区块链协会，不只是组织活动、连接资源，更是与伙伴一起建立共同目标，在具体工作中履行承诺，在面对分歧时保持尊重，在行业变化中坚持长期建设。回过头看，这些实践与《儒道RuDao》所强调的诚信、义利关系、和谐协作及长期主义，有着深刻的共鸣。

作为科技媒体创业者，我同样需要面对商业发展与价值坚持之间的选择。媒体既要保持对创新的敏感，也要珍惜读者的信任；既要帮助企业表达价值，也要守住内容的真实与责任。对我而言，"义利合一"不是回避商业利益，而是在追求发展时，不忘原则，不透支信用，也不牺牲合作伙伴的长期价值。

在我参与的 AI、Web3 与跨境合作项目中，许多交流始于线上，许多伙伴来自不同市场与文化背景。这让我更加重视一个问题:当人们不再依靠频繁的线下见面维系关系，合作靠什么持续？我的答案是清晰的共同目标、可信的行动，以及对承诺的认真对待。工具可以帮助我们连接，但信任仍需要在一次次合作中积累。

因此，我阅读《儒道RuDao》时，感受到的不只是传统思想的启发，也看到了自己与 Tony 哥多年共事经历中的一些坚持:以诚信作为合作的起点，以尊重作为沟通的基础，以共同成长作为关系的方向。他的谦逊与开放，也让我看到，书中倡导的精神不应只停留在文字里，更应体现在待人处事与日常行动中。

对我而言，《儒道RuDao》的意义，是让我们在讨论技术、效率与增长之外，重新思考事业的根基:我们为什么创业，如何对待伙伴，又希望建立怎样的长期价值。它也提醒我，真正值得投入的事业，不只是把自己做大，而是让更多人能够在可信的合作中共同向前。

我愿意向创业者、投资人、科技从业者及跨境合作的推动者推荐这本书。也期待继续与 Tony 哥及更多志同道合的伙伴一起，在媒体、行业社群与国际交流的实践中，将诚信、责任、合作与长期主义落实为行动。

— Alma 木木 TECHUB News 创始人 香港区块链协会HKBA副会长-秘书长

Sam Tam 譚耀泉 — A Letter of Recommendation · 一封推薦信

Chairman, Hong Kong SME Commerce and Industry Federation 香港中小企業工商聯合會 指導主席 · 3rd October 2026

致 各位商界同仁 · 企業家 · 投資人 · 創業者 —— To Fellow Business Leaders, Entrepreneurs, Investors & Founders:

今日，我謹向諸位鄭重推薦一本值得置於案頭、反覆重讀的好書——唐儀（TonyTong.cc）先生所著《儒道:儒將·超越孫子兵法》（RuDao : Beyond The Art of War）。

Today, I earnestly recommend to you a fine book worthy of a place on your desk and of repeated re-reading — RuDao: The Confucian General — Beyond The Art of War, by Mr. Tony Tong (唐儀).

先說作者。唐儀先生是真正「在戰場上打過仗」的人:生於桂林，少年赴港，成年赴美；他是跨境投資人與連續創業者，曾聯合創辦納斯達克上市企業 Pacificnet，持有美國互聯網工作流技術專利，亦是香港區塊鏈協會（HKBA）聯合創始人，並在多所大學擔任客座教授。更難得的，是他三十年穿越 Web 1.0 到 Web 4.0 的技術與商業浪潮，在功成名就之後，卻回頭向孔子、孟子、老子求取答案。這種「既懂刀兵、又返聖賢」的履歷，使這本書絕非書齋裡的空談。

Let me begin with the author. Mr. Tong is someone who has truly "seen battle": born in Guilin, he went to Hong Kong as a boy and to the United States as a young man. He is a cross-border investor and serial entrepreneur who co-founded the Nasdaq-listed company Pacificnet, holds a U.S. patent for internet workflow technology, and was a co-founder of the Hong Kong Blockchain Association (HKBA), while serving as a visiting professor at several universities. Rarer still, after three decades riding the waves of technology and business from Web 1.0 to Web 4.0 and achieving considerable success, he turned back to seek answers from Confucius, Mencius, and Laozi. This "versed in both warfare and the sages" background ensures the book is no mere ivory-tower talk.

這本書提出一個尖銳而溫柔的問題:在 AI 與區塊鏈主導商業的今天，我們為何還要讀兩千年前的聖賢？唐儀的回答，凝結為「儒道」二字——一套以「儒商」立本、以「儒將」致用、以「天人合一」貫通的經營之道（Way）。它不否定《孫子兵法》，卻清晰地指出了其邊界:零和對抗只能贏得一仗，而儒家的競合之道（Co-opetition）能贏得長久。書中以「五常五德」（仁、義、禮、智、信）為支柱，以「義利共存、競合並行、共贏聯盟」三原則為框架，向今天的領導者證明:你不必在利潤與原則之間二選一。

This book poses a sharp yet gentle question: in an era where AI and blockchain dominate commerce, why should we still read sages from two thousand years ago? Tong's answer condenses into two characters — "RuDao" (the Confucian Way): a way of doing business rooted in the "Confucian merchant" (RuShang), enacted through the "Confucian general" (RuJiang), and unified by the Taoist ideal of "heaven and humanity as one." It does not reject The Art of War, but clearly marks its boundary: zero-sum confrontation can win a single battle, whereas the Confucian path of co-opetition can win the long game. Built on the "Five Constant Virtues" (Ren, Yi, Li, Zhi, Xin — benevolence, righteousness, propriety, wisdom, trust) and framed by three principles — "righteousness and profit coexist," "competition and cooperation in parallel," and "win-win alliances" — it shows today's leaders that you need not choose between profit and principle.

「義，是敢於退出不該做的交易；信，是超越一切貨幣的貨幣。」在充斥短期博弈與破裂合夥關係的市場裡，這不僅是道德說教，更是可操作的商業紀律。

"Righteousness means the courage to walk away from deals you should not take; trust is a currency that transcends all others." In markets saturated with short-term games and broken partnerships, this is not moralizing but an operable business discipline.

最令我動容的，正是這種「以德為略、以信為基」的硬核長期主義。從子貢到稻盛和夫，從諸葛亮到現代儒將案例，唐儀用兩千五百年的脈絡證明:德性從來不是軟理想，而是最硬的策略。當他把儒家誠信放進 Web3 與 AI 治理的討論中時，你會忽然明白——先人的智慧，恰恰是我們今日技術困局的解答。

What moves me most is precisely this hard-edged long-termism of "virtue as strategy, trust as foundation." From Zigong to Kazuo Inamori, from Zhuge Liang to modern RuJiang case studies, Tong uses a 2,500-year thread to prove that virtue was never a soft ideal but the hardest strategy of all. When he brings Confucian integrity into the conversation on Web3 and AI governance, you suddenly realize — the wisdom of the ancients is precisely the answer to the technological predicaments of our day.

我特別想把這本書，推薦給三類人:

Three kinds of readers I especially recommend it to:

一、正帶領團隊擴張的企業家——它會給你一套「不必背叛自己也能贏」的方法論。

Entrepreneurs leading teams through expansion — it offers a methodology to "win without betraying yourself."

二、在海內外尋求夥伴的投資人與商業領袖——「競合並行、化敵為友」是比擊敗對手更高明的牌。

Investors and business leaders seeking partners at home and abroad — "compete and cooperate in parallel, turn rivals into friends" is a wiser hand than defeating opponents.

三、初出茅廬、想在商海中守住本心的年輕人——它提前告訴了你，三十年後你最想守住的到底是什麼。

Young people just starting out who wish to hold onto their true selves in the business world — it tells you in advance what you will most want to protect thirty years from now.

作為香港中小企業工商聯合會的指導主席，我長年與無數中小企業主並肩而行，對書中之道更有切身体会。中小企業要在巨頭林立的市場中求存致勝，靠的從不是把同行擠垮，而是把同業、供應商與客戶變成可信賴的長期盟友——這正是「競合並行、化敵為友」最質樸也最硬核的現實注腳。而書中所說的「信」，在香港這座以信用為基石的商業城邦裡，從來都是中小企業最值錢、也最經得起時間考驗的資產。

As the Guidance Chairman of the Hong Kong SME Commerce and Industry Federation, having stood shoulder to shoulder with countless small and medium enterprise owners over the years, I feel the book's teaching all the more keenly. For SMEs to survive and prevail in a market dominated by giants, success has never come from crushing competitors, but from turning peers, suppliers, and customers into trusted long-term allies — which is the most plain yet most hard-core real-world footnote to "compete and cooperate in parallel, turn rivals into friends." And the "trust" the book speaks of, in a commercial city like Hong Kong built on credit, has always been the SME's most valuable asset — the one that best withstands the test of time.

誠如作者所言:「RuDao 是我在起步時希望擁有的那本書。」我想補一句——它也是今天每一位華人商業領袖，都值得擁有的那本書。讀懂儒道，方知商道即人道；守住信義，方能走得既遠且穩。

As the author himself says: "RuDao is the book I wish I had had when I was starting out." I would add this — it is also a book every Chinese business leader today deserves to own. Understand RuDao, and you know that the way of business is the way of being human; keep faith and righteousness, and you can go far and steady.

以此為薦，願與諸君共讀、共行。

With this recommendation, may we read and walk this path together.

— 譚耀泉 Sam Tam · 香港中小企業工商聯合會 指導主席 · Chairman, Hong Kong SME Commerce and Industry Federation · 二〇二六年十月三日 3rd October 2026

Trevor Tong 唐樂庭

Vice Chairman, United World Chinese Association 世界華人協會副主席 · VP HKBA.net · Co-Founder, Tako AI & MintMind AI · CEO, Khemsiri Music 筠樂音樂

In a world where business literature often celebrates the sharpest elbows and the quickest exits, Tony's new book "RuDao - Beyond the Art of War" arrives as a profound and necessary corrective. This is not merely a book about strategy; it is a treatise on the soul of enterprise.

TonyTong writes not just as a seasoned global entrepreneur and investor, but as a true 儒商 (Confucian merchant) — one who embodies the belief that commerce and conscience are not opposing forces but complementary halves of a whole. He understands that the 儒 (Ru) spirit is not about weakness or passivity; it is about the quiet, unshakeable strength of moral clarity. It is the conviction that trust is the ultimate currency and that enduring partnerships are built on a foundation of virtue, not just contracts.

By integrating the 儒将 (scholar-general) tradition with the Taoist vision of 天人合一 (the unity of heaven and humanity), Tony offers a rare and elegant synthesis. He moves beyond the zero-sum mindset of The Art of War to propose a 儒将竞合战略 (co-opetition framework) — a way to compete fiercely without losing one's humanity, and to cooperate sincerely without losing one's edge.

RuDao is the book Tony wished he had when he started. It is also the book the next generation of global leaders desperately needs. I endorse it wholeheartedly, not just for its strategic insight, but for its deep and timely understanding of the 儒 spirit — a spirit that reminds us that the ultimate measure of success is not merely what we accumulate, but who we become in the process.

當今商管書籍往往推崇錙銖必較的權謀與急流勇退的捷徑，而 TonyTong 的《儒道 RuDao》問世，恰如一股清流，既是深刻的匡正，亦是必要的回歸。這不僅是一本關於策略的書，更是一部探討企業靈魂的論著。

TonyTong.cc 不僅以一位資深環球企業家及投資者的身份寫作，更以一位真正的「儒商」自居。他深信商業與良知並非對立，而是相輔相成的兩面。他深刻理解「儒」的精神並非軟弱或被動，而是一種源於道德清明的、靜水流深的堅韌力量。這是一種信念:信任是最終的貨幣，而長久的合作夥伴關係，是建立在德行而非僅僅是合約之上。

Tony 將「儒將」傳統與道家「天人合一」的洞見融會貫通，提出了罕見而優雅的綜合。他超越了《孫子兵法》的零和思維，開創出「儒將競合戰略」，一種既能激烈競爭而不失人性，又能真誠合作而不失鋒芒的平衡之道。

《儒道 RuDao》是 Tony 希望自己創業之初就能讀到的書，也是新一代全球領袖迫切需要的書。我由衷推薦此書，不僅因其戰略洞見，更因其對「儒」精神的深刻與適時的理解，這種精神提醒我們，成功的最終衡量標準，不僅在於我們積累了什麼，更在於我們在過程中成為了怎樣的人。

Wen Xiaojie 温曉傑 (温導 | 东方破晓)

Founder, Moushi 谋势体系 · 谋势人工智能(深圳)有限公司 www.AIsdfn.cn · Strategic Operator

研读《孙子兵法 Art of War》多年，市面上诸多商战解读，大多局限于博弈击敌、求一时之胜。展读唐儀先生《儒道 RuDao》，读后深为触动，视角格局卓尔不凡，堪称数字商业时代极具启发性的战略力作。

I have studied The Art of War for many years. Most business interpretations of it focus narrowly on outmaneuvering rivals and chasing short-term victory. After reading Tony Tong's The Confucian Way, I was deeply impressed by its exceptional perspective and vision. It is an extraordinarily insightful strategic work for the era of digital commerce.

追溯本源，《孙子兵法》最高要义，不在攻伐取胜，而在:善战者，求之于势，不责于人。这句话，正是我创立【谋势 Moushi】体系的思想原点。世间操盘手皆知造势借势，懂得蓄能布局、顺势取利；却极易陷入一种惯性认知:商战即是势能碾压，竞争就是零和对局。

The supreme teaching of The Art of War is not merely about defeating foes. It states: "The skilled warrior seeks victory from the momentum of circumstances, not by demanding results from people." This is the foundational philosophy behind my Moushi Framework. Many seasoned operators understand how to build and leverage momentum, design layouts and seize gains when the timing favors them. Even so, many fall into a habitual mindset: business competition is simply the overpowering of rivals, a zero-sum contest.

势能固然是破局制胜的核心。可再强的势能，如果缺少立信、结盟、共生的底层支撑，博弈越激烈，信任消耗越快，再宏大的局，也容易快速溃散。古谚有云:买卖不成仁义在。真正高阶的战略，是既能造大势、决胜负，亦能守道义、建同盟。在全球化、AI 驱动，瞬息万变的数字生态里，如何驭势而不困于势，在竞争之外培育长期伙伴生态，走向可持续的共赢，我理解，这便是唐儀先生落笔著述的初心。

Momentum is undoubtedly the core engine for breaking deadlocks and winning. Yet even formidable momentum, without the foundation of trust, alliance and coexistence, will erode trust amid fierce rivalry. No matter how grand the setup, it can unravel quickly. An ancient maxim tells us: "Even if the deal fails, benevolence and righteousness remain." The highest level of strategy means being capable of building massive momentum and winning decisive contests, while also upholding integrity and forming alliances. Within the fast-shifting, AI-powered global digital ecosystem, the challenge is to command momentum without being trapped by it, to nurture long-term partnerships beyond pure competition and pursue sustainable win-win outcomes. I believe this is Mr. Tony Tong's core inspiration for writing this book.

科技迭代提速，跨境生态持续扩张，市场维度愈发复杂。只懂对抗的旧范式，已经很难穿越周期。《儒道》恰逢其时，为新一代战略操盘手升维破局，指明长期经营的大道。

Technology evolves at a rapid pace, cross-border ecosystems keep expanding, and market complexity continues to rise. Old models built only on confrontation struggle to endure market cycles. The Confucian Way arrives at exactly the right time, offering next-level strategic thinking and illuminating the enduring path for modern operators.

追溯典籍，《孙子兵法》长期被商界奉为战略圭臬，无数操盘者依此谋局布局。

For generations, The Art of War has stood as the gold standard of business strategy, relied on by countless operators to map and execute their plays.

《儒道 RuDao》尊重兵家智慧，不否定造势驭势的价值，引述经典，层层递进。取孙子"善战求势"的顶层心法，教会我们审慎布局，蓄势待发，在博弈之中寻找共存共生的可能；融入儒门仁义礼智信，倡导竞合之道。书中直指商业博弈常见的误区:以击败对手为唯一目标的零和思维，会侵蚀信任，摧毁合作根基，难以维系长期稳定的伙伴关系。《儒道 · 儒将》跳出单纯对抗式商战逻辑，阐释根植于儒道思想的竞合范式:利润与信义本非二元对立，激烈竞争的同时，一样可以搭建稳固长久的共生网络。这，正是本书独特的价值。

The Confucian Way honors Sun Tzu's wisdom and does not dismiss the power of strategic momentum. It builds its argument step by step, drawing from ancient classics. It adopts Sun Tzu's supreme principle of seeking victory through momentum, teaching us to plan prudently, build energy, and seek coexistence alongside competition. It integrates the Confucian virtues of benevolence, righteousness, propriety, wisdom and trustworthiness to advocate co-opetition. The book calls out a pervasive flaw in commercial gaming: zero-sum thinking that prioritizes beating competitors corrodes trust, undermines cooperation, and makes durable partnerships nearly impossible. The Confucian Way - The Confucian General transcends confrontational business warfare. It lays out a co-opetition model rooted in Confucian thought: profit and integrity do not need to be opposites. Even amid fierce rivalry, stable, long-term collaborative networks can be built. This is the book's singular contribution.

唐儀 TonyTong 先生层层铺陈，核心主张，并非否定逐利的合理性，而是提醒世人:利有价，信义无价。追逐商业回报，与守护长期信义，可以兼得。能驭大势，亦能守大道，才是战略家的至高境界。这并非刻意标新立异，而是长期浸润一线商业，对真实市场生态沉淀出的深刻洞见。

Mr. Tony Tong develops his argument carefully. He does not dismiss profit-seeking as inherently wrong. Instead, he reminds us: financial gains carry measurable value, yet integrity and trust are priceless. It is possible to pursue commercial returns while preserving long-term trust. Mastering momentum while honoring the higher Way marks the apex of strategic mastery. This is no gimmick or fleeting idea; it is hard-earned wisdom forged through long observation of real business ecosystems.

我深为认同，鼎力推荐《儒道 RuDao》。

I share this vision wholeheartedly, and therefore I strongly recommend The Confucian Way.

— Wen Xiaojie 温曉傑 (温導 | 东方破晓) · 战略操盘手, 谋势 Moushi 体系创始人 · 谋势人工智能(深圳)有限公司 www.AIsdfn.cn

Professor Zeng Qingyu 曾庆宇教授

APEC Energy Expert 能源专家 · General Advisor, International Federation of Digital Economy Ecology 数字经济生态国际联合会总顾问 · 国务院特殊津贴专家 · 多家上市公司战略顾问

唐儀先生所著《儒道》一书，尚未付梓前，我就通过各种方式零星拜读过部分章节。读后耳目一新，为他论商的著作立意、撰写角度拍手叫好，是当前商战的一本谋略好书奇书。

Before Mr. TonyTong's The Confucian Way was formally published, I had already read parts of it here and there through various channels. After reading it, I felt refreshed and delighted, applauding the conceptual approach and writing angle of his work on business. It is a brilliant and extraordinary book of strategy for today's business battles.

追根溯源，我国自商朝始，商业、商品、商人便初显端倪，如是就有了商丘之城。成就了三商之源，华商之都。百家三商于斯诞生，诸子思想产出之源头。古往今来，商末周初，姜子牙就有言:天下熙熙，皆为利来；天下攘攘，皆为利往。一语道出人性真谛。民间也有言:无利不起早；商家也归纳出:只有永恒的利益，没有永恒的朋友。简言之，经商，就是商战，就是利益驱使的人类普遍自然行为。图利牟利是一种极正常的人生准则，上千年来就如此。当然也有例外，典型的就是越国大夫范蠡，传说曾三次散尽巨财。但这毕竟是凤毛麟角。

Tracing back to its origins, China's business, commodities, and merchants first began to take shape as early as the Shang Dynasty, and thus the city of Shangqiu came into being. It became the source of the "Three Shang" and the capital of Chinese merchants. The Hundred Schools of Thought and the Three Shang were born here, and it was also a source of the emergence of various philosophical schools. From ancient times to the transition between the Shang and Zhou dynasties, Jiang Ziya once said: "All under heaven bustle for profit; all under heaven rush for profit." This single statement reveals the true nature of human beings. There is also a folk saying: "No one rises early without profit." Merchants have likewise concluded: "There are only eternal interests, not eternal friends." In short, doing business is commercial warfare; it is a universal natural human behavior driven by interests. Seeking and pursuing profit is an entirely normal principle of life, and it has been so for thousands of years. Of course, there are exceptions. A typical one is Fan Li, the senior official of the State of Yue, who is said to have scattered enormous fortunes three times. But this is, after all, extremely rare.

《儒道 RuDao》，一改数千年来人们过往的认知。用"仁义道德、寡廉鲜耻"来衡量当下从商树德的标准。简单的解读，就是用儒家的道德标准来度量商人行为准则:商人即是竞争对手，更应成为相互激励的朋友。古人有言:买卖不成仁义在。在竞争中，尽量以儒家仁义礼智信基本道德品行守住经营竞争底线，制造和谐共赢的历史画面，在当今市场信息高速运转、瞬息万变的状态下，如何把控机遇，推进社会更高层次的发展进步，真正"和天下"，我揣磨这应该就是唐儀先生著书立说的初衷本意。

The Confucian Way changes the understanding people have had for thousands of years. It uses "benevolence, righteousness, morality, and a sense of shame" to measure the standard by which today's merchants should establish their virtue. Simply interpreted, it uses Confucian moral standards to measure the behavioral principles of merchants: merchants are competitors, but they should also become friends who encourage one another. The ancients said: "Even if the deal fails, benevolence and righteousness remain." In competition, one should try to use the basic Confucian moral qualities of benevolence, righteousness, propriety, wisdom, and trustworthiness to hold the bottom line of business competition, creating a historical picture of harmonious and win-win outcomes. In today's market, where information circulates at high speed and changes in an instant, how can one grasp opportunities, promote social development and progress at a higher level, and truly achieve "peace under heaven"? I surmise that this should be Mr. TonyTong's original intention and true purpose in writing the book.

时代大进步，科技更尖端，信息变速快，市场更辽阔，更需要商家之间的紧密合作与相互尊重去应对多维市场。《儒道》这本专著应运而生，就起到了画龙点睛、指点迷径、擘画乾坤的作用。

The times are advancing greatly, technology is more sophisticated, information changes rapidly, and the market is broader. Merchants therefore need even closer cooperation and mutual respect to respond to a multidimensional market. The Confucian Way, this monograph, has emerged at the right moment, playing the role of adding the finishing touch, pointing out the way, and laying out a grand vision.

追溯历史，《孙子兵法》不单是兵家推崇的宝典，也被商家视为圭臬、不二法宝。

Tracing history, The Art of War by Sun Tzu is not only a treasured classic admired by military strategists, but has also been regarded by merchants as a standard and an infallible magic weapon.

而《儒道》这本书尊重历史典籍，不排斥先人名言戒律，而是引经据典，以孙子兵法:知己知彼，百战不殆不打无准备的商战，提醒商家认真对待每一次交锋，与竞争对手共存共赢；以孔孟思想:己所不欲，勿施于人要与人为善的交易，提醒商家与人方便与己方便，方能商道畅通。如何一反常态，修正数千年来人们固有认知与观念，即就是唐儀先生著作中关于《儒商-儒将》论述所言:"往往作为商业博弈的经典，以击败竞争者为目标的零和思维，所以常常摧毁合作根基、损耗信任，难以维系长期伙伴关系。《儒道-儒将》跳出单纯对抗的商战逻辑，阐释源自儒家思想的竞合之道；不必在利润和情义之间二选一，在激烈竞争的同时，依然可以构建稳固、长久的伙伴网络。"这无疑是唐儀先生著书立说的目的及意义所在。

The Confucian Way respects historical classics and does not reject the famous sayings and precepts of the ancients. Instead, it cites classics and references sources. Using The Art of War's "Know yourself and know your enemy, and you will never be defeated in a hundred battles," it reminds merchants to take every business battle seriously and not fight unprepared, and to coexist and win together with competitors. Using Confucian and Mencian thought — "Do not do to others what you do not want done to yourself" — it reminds merchants to trade with kindness, for only by making things convenient for others can one's own path be convenient, and only then can the business path remain open. How can one reverse the usual state of affairs and correct the fixed cognition and concepts people have held for thousands of years? This is precisely what Mr. Tang Yi discusses in his work regarding "Confucian Merchant - Confucian General": "What is often taken as the classic approach to business gaming is zero-sum thinking aimed at defeating competitors. Therefore, it often destroys the foundation of cooperation, wears away trust, and makes long-term partnerships difficult to sustain. The Confucian Way - Confucian General jumps out of the simple confrontational logic of commercial warfare and explains the way of competition and cooperation derived from Confucian thought: one need not choose between profit and righteousness; while competing fiercely, one can still build a stable and lasting partnership network." This is undoubtedly the purpose and significance of Mr. TonyTong's book.

TonyTong.cc 唐儀先生洋洋洒洒数千言 RuDao.org，目的无非是劝人向善。告诫人们:情义比利益更重要。利益是有价价值，情谊是无价之宝，情义续存才是人生完美真谛。在获得人生所追求利益的同时，更不可丢失情义，二者完全可以兼而有之，这才是人生完美得意巅峰。此观点，决不是唐儀先生标新立异，心血来潮之举，而是实实在在观摩大千世界的感悟与心得。

Mr. Tony Tong writes at great length, and his purpose is nothing other than to persuade people to do good. He warns people: righteousness and affection are more important than profit. Profit has measurable value, while friendship and righteousness are priceless treasures. The continuation of righteousness and affection is the true essence of a perfect life. While obtaining the interests one pursues in life, one must not lose righteousness and affection. The two can absolutely be had at the same time. This is the peak of a perfect and successful life. This viewpoint is by no means Mr. Tang Yi's attempt to be novel and unconventional or a passing impulse, but rather a genuine insight and reflection gained from observing the vast world.

我钦服并认同，所以我竭力推荐《儒道》。

I admire and agree with it, and therefore I strongly recommend The Confucian Way.

— Professor Zeng Qingyu 曾庆宇教授 · 国务院特殊津贴专家, APEC 能源专家, 数字经济生态国际联合会总顾问, 多家上市公司战略顾问

Dr. Yibiao Chen 陈宜飚博士

Former CEO & CIO, SPIC HK Asset Management 国家电投香港资管前 CEO 兼 CIO

唐仪（TonyTong.cc）先生的新著《RuDao: Beyond the Art of War》（儒道:超越孙子兵法），是一部跨越 2500 年历史、贯通东西方商业智慧的里程碑式著作。全书系统阐述了儒商精髓，破局性地提出从「零和博弈」走向「竞合共赢」的战略框架，并辅以中日经典商业案例，可谓是从孔孟古训到 AI 与 Web3 时代，探索商业永续与共赢的全新战略典范！

本书不仅深入解析了「义以生利」与「民无信不立」在现代跨境联盟与危机领导力中的应用，更前瞻性地将儒家德性伦理引入 AI 商业治理、Web3 信任机制与 DAO 通证经济。基于作者 30 年横跨 Web1.0 至 Web4 浪潮的实战沉淀，本书为全球企业家、投资人与科技创新者提供了一份兼具思想深度与实战可操作性的「现代儒商与儒将指南」。

— 陈宜飚 博士 · 前国家电投香港资管 CEO 兼 CIO · 香港国际金融学会资深会员兼《香港国际金融评论》执行编辑 · Dr. Yibiao Chen, Former CEO & CIO, SPIC HK Asset Management, Senior Fellow of HKIIF & Executive Editor of HKIFR

Hu XuanFeng 胡烜峰

FoSun Wealth 复星财富数字资产董事 · 长三角区块链产业促进中心副主任 · Co-author of Stablecoin & AI

Ramble

Managing Partner, AlphaGrace Limited · Founder, GenAI Summit

After years of investing in AI in Silicon Valley, I've come to believe one thing: the more powerful our technology becomes, the scarcer trust gets. RuDao brings 2,500 years of Confucian wisdom on co-opetition into the age of AI and Web3. It is not a sermon. It is a practical discipline for turning rivals into partners and building relationships that outlast the deal. Drawing on thirty years across four technology waves, Tony Tong has written a book for every founder and investor who wants to win for the long term, not just win the next round.

在硅谷投资 AI 的这几年，我越来越确信一件事:技术越强大，信任越稀缺。当算法开始替人谈判、替人交易，真正拉开差距的，是谁能让伙伴愿意长久同行，而不是谁更会算计对手。唐仪先生的《儒道》，把两千五百年的儒家竞合智慧，带进了 AI 与 Web3 的现实战场。这本书讲的不是道德说教，是一套经得起周期考验的经营方法:义利可以合一，对手可以成为伙伴。从香港到硅谷，从 Web1 到 Web4，作者以三十年亲历写就此书。推荐给每一位想赢得长久、而不只是赢下一局的创业者和投资人。

— Ramble · AlphaGrace Limited 管理合伙人 · GenAI Summit 发起人 · Managing Partner, AlphaGrace Limited · Founder, GenAI Summit

XiaoYi EMBA

Cheung Kong Graduate School of Business 长江商学院 CKGSB.edu.cn

孔子曰:「志于道，据于德，依于仁，游于艺」；老子曰:「道可道，非常道」。这两句古老箴言，一者指向人如何在世间立身行事，一者提醒人真正的根本之道往往超越言辞与条框。儒字拆开，乃「人之需」，也就是如何做人，而其核心思想，正是一个「仁」字。

唐仪 TonyTong.cc 先生以多年跨国商业实践为基础，在《儒道 RuDao》中，将这一「仁」字放回具体的商业关系之中，认真面对利益分歧与竞争压力，让古老智慧不再停留在书斋之中，而是进入真实、复杂、充满变数的商业现场。

商业世界从不缺少利益，也不缺少竞争。真正困难的，是利润追求与长久伙伴关系之间如何平衡。合作的难处，往往不在初识，而在利益变化之后:能否兑现承诺，如何分配所得，又能否为对方保留继续合作的空间。许多联盟在顺境中看似牢固，一旦市场波动、利润缩减、权责变化，便容易生出猜疑与裂痕。书中以「义利合一」为根基，指出道义与利益本非对立。义不是利益的否定，而是利益的边界与长久保障；利也不是义的对立面，而是义在现实中的落地与检验。唯有在利益变动之际仍以仁义相待，方能使联盟经得起风浪。

在 AI 与区块链蓬勃发展的时代，许多伙伴无需线下会面，异国法律与文化之间，信任何以建立？老子言「道可道，非常道」，提醒我们真正的治理不在繁复条框，而在顺应自然的默契；孔子之「仁」，则让远距离协作始终照见「人之需」。技术可以提升效率，可以降低成本，可以跨越地域，但技术不能自动生成信任。信任仍要回到人本身:是否尊重对方，是否守诺，是否在分歧中仍留余地，是否在竞争中不失底线。

这是一本将中华古老智慧注入当代跨境远程联盟的思想力作。它不提供僵化的公式，而是提供一种方向:犹如行于大道，既要有「志于道」的理想牵引，也要有「游于艺」的从容应变。它让读者看到，儒与道并非远在庙堂或山林，而可以进入合同、谈判、分配、协作与危机处理之中。这本书给予全球创业者的，不仅是一套治理的方法，更是一种让人心安定的力量——在变幻莫测的商业世界里，仍知道何以为人，何以为伴，何以为道。

— XiaoYi EMBA · Cheung Kong Graduate School of Business 长江商学院 · CKGSB.edu.cn

Christina J ZHAO

Founder, Twinkle FO · Scholar of International Affairs Strategy 国际事务战略学者

RuDao – Beyond The Art of War is a masterpiece that synthesizes 2,500 years of Confucian wisdom with the hard realities of modern global commerce. The author does not discard Sun Tzu, but absorbs and transcends him — guiding readers out of zero-sum thinking and into the higher-dimensional strategic art of co-opetition. This work offers practical and actionable paradigms of cross-cultural cooperation, providing valuable inspiration for international strategic studies and the implementation of diplomatic engagement. In an era defined by AI, blockchain, and borderless remote collaboration, the book provides every entrepreneur, investor, and cross-border leader with an indispensable "North Star": a framework where profit and principle are no longer opposing forces, but twin engines driving enduring success.

This work deserves a place on the shelves of the world's great libraries. At Maugham Library (King's College London), it belongs equally in Military Science — as a necessary evolution beyond The Art of War — and in Economics & Political Philosophy, as a living classic text for the digital century. At the National Library of China (Beijing), at this very moment of reconstructing political orthodoxy amid the "Great Changes Unseen in Three Thousand Years," advancing economic transformation amid the "Profound Changes Unseen in a Century," and urgently reshaping Responsible AI amid the fragmentation of the international order, this book stands as both a profound retrospective and contemporary distillation of political economy and legal philosophy, and an indispensable field manual for entrepreneurs navigating complex markets with integrity.

A timely, profound, and deeply humane guide — I recommend it without reservation.

《儒道——超越孙子兵法》是一部融汇两千五百年儒家思想与现代全球商业实战的杰作。作者并未否定孙武，而是将其融会贯通，引领读者从零和博弈的思维定式中走出，步入「竞合」这一更高维度的战略艺术。本书为国际战略思想研究与外交互动落地，提供了切实可行的跨文化合作范式启发。在人工智能、区块链与无界远程协作的时代，本书为每一位创业者、投资人与跨境商业领袖提供了不可或缺的「北极星」指引——一套让利润与道义不再对立、而是并驾齐驱驱动长远成功的思维体系。

此作理应被收藏于世界顶级图书馆。在伦敦国王学院莫恩图书馆（Maugham Library），它既可归入军事科学类——作为《孙子兵法》的必要演进；亦可列入经济与政治哲学类——成为数字时代鲜活的经典文本。而在当今中国国家图书馆（北京），值此「三千未有之大变局」的政治道统重塑、「百年未有之大变局」的经济主题建设，以及国际秩序失序下负责任 AI（Responsible AI）重点重塑生发之际，本书无疑既是政治经济学与法哲学的深刻回溯与时代精髓升华，更是企业家在复杂市场中以诚信致胜的实战参考宝典。

这是一部及时、深刻且充满人文温度的指南——我毫无保留地推荐。

— Christina J ZHAO · Founder, Twinkle FO & Scholar of International Affairs Strategy · Twinkle FO Founder & 国际事务战略学者

Wu SiJin 吴思进

Blockchain Developer · Hangzhou, China

Blockchain's mission is to rebuild the scarcest resource of our age: Trust. It shifts trust from "relying on people" to "relying on rules" — data on-chain, tamper-proof, fully traceable. For the first time, trust becomes social infrastructure. Traceability leaves inferior goods nowhere to hide; on-chain records make fraud and fake traffic no longer worth the cost. Intermediaries no longer live off the "trust premium," and companies no longer survive by harvesting traffic — the attention economy gives way to the value economy, and wealth is created through technology and management rather than transferred through hype and zero-sum games.

This is the most profound change of all: only when fraud stops paying and speculation stops rewarding will capital and talent flow to endeavors that genuinely advance society and technology — positive-sum creation replacing zero-sum games. Code writes trust on-chain; virtue must be cultivated off-chain. RuDao restores trust to the digital world — and conscience to commerce.

区块链的使命，是重建这个时代最稀缺的东西——信任。它把信任从「对人」变成「对规则」:数据上链、不可篡改、全程可溯，信任第一次成为社会基础设施。溯源让以次充好无处遁形，链上留痕让刷量造假得不偿失；中介不再靠「信任差价」吃饭，企业不再靠「流量收割」生存——流量经济让位于价值经济，财富的创造从「忽悠与博弈」回归「科技与管理」。

这才是最深刻的变革:当欺诈无利可图、投机不再暴利，资本与人才才会流向真正推动社会技术进步的事业——零和博弈让位于正和创造。链上写下信任，链下修出德性，《儒道》为数字文明立信，为商业文明立心。

— Wu SiJin 吴思进 · Blockchain Developer · Hangzhou, China

Zhai Qing 翟青

Massey University

写《孙子兵法》的商战书很多，教人赢的很多，TonyTong 唐仪先生的《儒道 RuDao》却问了一个更难得的问题:赢下这一仗之后呢？

作者以三十年穿越 Web1.0 到 Web4.0 的亲身实践作答:击败对手是零和，化敌为友才是正和；利润与道义不必二选一，义利合一，方能长久。全书三篇十八章，从儒商八德讲到儒将谈判与危机，再到 AI 治理与 Web3 时代的信任机制——不卖弄玄虚，每一章都落到谈判桌、合同与危机处理之中。

我尤其认同书中「义利共存、竞合并行、共赢联盟」三大原则。算法越强大，信任越稀缺；把对手变成伙伴，把一单生意做成长期交情，才是这个时代最硬的竞争力。

这本书不是道德说教，而是一套经得起周期考验的经营纪律。推荐给每一位想把事业做长久的人。

TonyTong's new book RuDao asks the question few business books dare to: what comes after the victory?

For thirty years across four technology waves — from Web1.0 to Web4.0 — TonyTong has lived the answer. Moving beyond The Art of War's zero-sum mindset, he shows that the greater game is co-opetition: compete where it matters, cooperate where it counts, and build partnerships that outlast the deal. Profit and principle are not trade-offs — righteousness generates profit, and trust is the one asset that compounds and never depreciates.

Structured in three parts and eighteen chapters — from the eight virtues of the Confucian merchant to the negotiation and crisis leadership of the Confucian strategist, and on to AI governance and trust in the Web3 era — this is no book of slogans. It is an operating discipline for enduring business.

In an age when algorithms grow more powerful and trust grows scarcer, RuDao offers something precious: a way to win that does not require betraying yourself. I recommend it to every founder, investor, and leader who wants to win for the long term — and to turn rivals into partners.

— Zhai Qing 翟青 · Massey University

Chen JingSi 陈静思

HongKong Family Office 香港比元家族办公室

商场不是战场。《儒道 RuDao》把《孙子兵法 The Art of War》"击败对手"的零和思维，翻转为儒家"化敌为友"的竞合框架——义利合一、盟友比敌人值钱。在 AI 与 Web3 重塑信任的今天，它给了企业家一种真正能走得远的赢法。

— Chen JingSi 陈静思 · HongKong Family Office 香港比元家族办公室

Zoro 洪巍

OPC AI Game Maker Developer · Singapore

以儒道立心，以共赢行远！

世人论成事之道，常推崇兵家谋略。TonyTong's RuDao.org《儒道-超越孙子兵法 Beyond the Art of War》让我读懂:竞争能让人一时立足，长久之道却不能止于争胜，更在于儒道相融、义利共生。

若将世事都视作战场，一味争利、算计输赢，便容易消耗信任、透支人心。赢了眼前的较量，未必赢得长远的发展。人与人之间，不只有对抗，也有相互成就的可能。

儒家以仁义礼智信为立身之本，教人守底线、重诚信、知分寸；道家强调顺应自然，教人尊重规律、懂得克制、不妄强求。二者相融，让竞争有边界，让获利有原则。

儒道的处世之道，并非放弃竞争、处处退让，而是不把击败对手当作唯一目标，更重视创造价值、成就彼此。义与利也并非对立:坚守道义不是舍弃利益，而是拒绝贪婪短视与投机取巧，让利益建立在信任与合作之上。

在互联共生的时代，人与人、行业与行业日益紧密相连。AI拓展了协作的可能，也让我们更需要跳出单打独斗的思维。比起独占眼前的利益，建立互惠、可信、可持续的合作，更能支撑长久的事业。

以儒道立心，竞争不失本心，获利不失道义；以共赢行远，不执着于一时输赢，而致力于共同成长。

— Zoro 洪巍 · OPC AI Game Maker Developer · Singapore

Guo XinFeng 郭新峰

Chairman, Zhongbiaolian Inspection, Testing and Certification Group 中标联检验检测认证集团有限公司创始人 · Executive Dean, Huaxia Standardization Research Institute 华夏标院执行院长

"商场不应是毫无底线的战场，而应是共同建立规则的竞技场。作为深耕检验检测认证与标准化领域多年的从业者，我深知'标准'与'信任'才是商业长青的基石。唐仪先生 TonyTong 的《RuDao 儒道》极具前瞻性地提出了'儒家竞合之道'，这与现代标准化体系和合规精神不谋而合。它超越了《孙子兵法》传统的零和博弈，倡导以仁义与规则平衡利润，构建长久的伙伴关系。这是一本为当代企业家提供战略定力与东方智慧的必读佳作，我隆重推荐!"

很高兴收到香港好友 唐仪 TonyTong 的新书《RuDao 儒道》。光看封面（竹简、围棋、阴阳太极）就已觉东方美学拉满。作为在检验检测认证和标准化领域摸爬滚打多年的创业者，我尤其认同书中"超越孙子兵法，以儒家竞合之道平衡利润与长久伙伴关系"的理念。在当今内卷严重、零和博弈盛行的商业环境下，Tony 没有教大家如何"打败对手"，而是回归儒家智慧，提出了"竞合 Co-opetition"。这和我们做标准、做认证的初衷完全一致——用规则建立信任，在合作中共赢。这种既竞争又合作、追求长期主义的格局，非常值得每一位创业者和管理者深思。祝 Tony 新书大卖！强烈推荐给大家！

作为中标联检验检测认证集团的创始人和华夏标院的执行院长，我始终坚信:"标准是世界的通用语言，信任是商业的基石。"唐仪先生 TonyTong 的《RuDao 儒道》深刻剖析了这一商业命题。书中提出的"儒家竞合之道"（The Confucian Co-opetition Way），是对传统商业竞争思维的一次升华。它告诉我们，真正的商业成功，不在于击败对手，而在于在规则与仁义之下，实现利润平衡与长久伙伴关系。在检验检测认证行业，我们依靠公正的规则和标准来促成企业间的"竞合"，这与《RuDao》的核心理念高度一致。这本书不仅是对《孙子兵法》的超越，更是对当代商业文明的深刻思考。我郑重向广大企业家、管理者和朋友们推荐这本书，愿我们在华夏智慧的指引下，共同构建一个更加共赢、可持续的商业未来。

— Guo XinFeng 郭新峰 · Chairman of Zhongbiaolian Inspection, Testing and Certification Group Co., Ltd. 中标联检验检测认证集团有限公司 · Executive Dean, Huaxia Standardization Research Institute 华夏标院执行院长

Fu Rao 付饒

CEO, RWA.Ltd · 香港國際新經濟研究院 INERI.net

世人多以《孫子兵法》講商戰博弈，卻容易陷入零和對抗的思維。《RuDao 儒道:超越孫子兵法》立足東方智慧，借儒家義利合一之道，探討如何平衡企業利潤與長久夥伴關係。跳脫兵學的勝敗邏輯，以和合共生的視角，為現代經營提供可參照的競合思路。

商戰不只有兵者詭道的博弈，更有儒道和合的長遠格局。本書立足東方智慧，跳脫《孫子兵法》零和對抗的戰爭視角，以儒家「義利合一、和而不同」重構當代競合邏輯。教企業管理者在現實利潤與長久夥伴關係之間取得動態平衡，把東方經典智慧轉化為組織經營、生態共建的實踐方案，是面向不確定時代的東方商道思考。

— Fu Rao 付饒 · CEO RWA.Ltd · 香港國際新經濟研究院 INERI.net

在《儒道 RuDao》中，TonyTong.cc 以深邃的东方智慧为骨、现代战略思维为脉，将儒道精髓从古老典籍中解救出来，赋予其应对当代复杂挑战的鲜活生命力。这不仅仅是一本关于"道"的书，更是一套可实践、可体悟的生活与决策框架。无论你是探寻东方智慧的读者，还是在现实中寻找战略锚点的实践者，这本书都将成为你书架上不可或缺的座右之选。

Wang Xiuhua 王秀華

Honorary Chair 榮譽主席, China-AnChu.com 中国安储 · RWA International Society RWA生態國際聯合會 資產數字化生態國際總會 · WeRWA.co

I recommend RuDao by TonyTong.cc. It is a work that truly brings Confucian wisdom into the modern business arena. Drawing on his own cross-border business experience, the author directly confronts the tensions between profit and morality, competition and cooperation, and strategy and ethics, offering a framework for corporate decision-making and partnerships that balances ethical responsibility with competitive strength. What is even more valuable is that the book does not stop at a simple grafting of tradition onto modernity. Instead, it places the Confucian virtues of benevolence, righteousness, propriety, wisdom, and trustworthiness within new technological contexts such as AI governance, Web3, and the token economy, translating them into practical financial principles and collaborative norms. It shows that pursuing profit and building lasting cooperative relationships are not contradictory; truly outstanding business means upholding morality, earning trust, and achieving win-win outcomes amid changing technologies and markets. I recommend it to every corporate decision-maker, entrepreneur, and anyone concerned with business ethics and the future of technology.

推荐 唐仪 TonyTong.cc 的《儒道 RuDao.org》。这是一部把儒家智慧真正带入现代商业现场的作品。作者以自身跨境商业实践为底稿，直面利润与道义、竞争与合作、策略与伦理之间的张力，为企业决策与伙伴关系提供了一套兼顾伦理与竞争力的思考框架。更难得的是，它没有停留在传统与现代的简单嫁接，而是把"仁义礼智信"置于人工智能治理、Web3与Token经济等新技术语境中，转化为可实践的金融原则与协作准则。它让人看到:追求利润与建立持久合作关系并不矛盾，真正高明的商业，是在变化的技术与市场里守住道义、赢得信任、实现共赢。推荐给每一位企业决策者、创业者和关心商业伦理与科技未来的人。

Austin X Huang, Ph.D. P.E., BcGE, F.ASCE

Principal, Merit Engineering, Inc. MeritEngineering.com · Seattle, Washington, USA

After observing decades of shifting market cycles, I often wonder why many businesses achieve fleeting success yet few stand the test of time. Tony Tong's "RuDao - Beyond the Art of War" draws from traditional Chinese philosophy and offers thoughtful perspectives that align with my real world business experience.

Most modern business writing emphasizes quick tactics and short term gains, but such profits rarely endure. Rather than merely repeating ancient proverbs, RuDao reinterprets classic wisdom for today's digital economy. It pushes leaders to rethink common assumptions around competition, profit motives, and corporate social accountability.

The book rejects zero sum business mindsets. Profit is essential for survival, yet it should not be an organization's only goal. Long term resilience relies on ethical judgment, mutual respect with partners, and consistent trust building. Authentic credibility cannot be bought by marketing; it grows from principled choices, even under pressure for instant results.

There are no get rich quick solutions here. Instead, RuDao provides a valuable framework for running businesses with both commercial competence and strong ethics, pursuing growth that benefits more than just shareholders. For entrepreneurs and investors navigating constant market disruption, this is essential reading.

— Austin X Huang, Ph.D. P.E., BcGE, F.ASCE · Principal, Merit Engineering, Inc., MeritEngineering.com, Seattle, Washington, USA
